# ***DRAFT*** Gemini Enterprise for CX Security Requirements

!!! warning "Draft Content"
    This content is currently in draft and may change before publication


## Overview

This Gemini Enterprise for CX Security Guide identifies the minimum requirements that must be met for the use of Gemini Enterprise for CX. This guide helps fulfill our commitment to protecting company and customer data and ensuring compliance with legal and regulatory requirements.

### Scope

This configuration guide is applicable to all usage of Gemini Enterprise for CX by Highmark Inc, its subsidiaries, and partners unless otherwise stated.

### Minimum Requirements

This document presents the minimum-security requirements that must be met to use Gemini Enterprise for CX. Individual entities, services, or systems may implement security controls above and beyond those specified herein. Those additional controls may augment and enhance, but may never reduce, the level of security specified by this guide.

### Definitions

This document may use terms and acronyms defined in the following sources:

- [General Cloud Glossary](../../glossaryGeneral.md)
- [GCP Glossary](../../gcp/glossaryGCP.md)
- [Azure Glossary](../../azure/glossaryAzure.md)
- [AWS Glossary](../../aws/glossaryAWS.md)

### Guidance Terms

This guidance uses the terms **SHOULD**, **SHOULD NOT**, **MAY**, **SHALL**, **MUST** and **MUST NOT** as defined [here](../../guidanceTerms.md).

### Documentation and Exception Requirements

If unable to follow the provided guidance proper documentation and/or a formal exception is required.

The documentation and exception requirements can be found [here](../../deviationProcess.md)

---

## Security Requirements

## General

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000010.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | Features of Gemini Enterprise for CX that are Non-GA or Pre-GA **MUST NOT** be used in production or with protected health information or other personal data. |
| Rationale | Any capability not designated GA is governed by Google's Pre-GA Offerings Terms in the Service Specific Terms. Those terms state that Pre-GA offerings are provided "as is", may be changed, suspended, or discontinued without prior notice, are not covered by any SLA, and, unless Google documentation expressly says otherwise, are not covered by the Data Location section or by any data processing terms. They also state that "Customer should not use Pre-GA Offerings to process personal data or other data subject to legal or regulatory compliance requirements." Google's HIPAA guidance says separately: "Do not use Pre-GA offerings ... in connection with PHI". A Pre-GA capability handling member conversation content therefore sits outside the contractual perimeter the BAA establishes. The exposure is created by production reliance and by regulated data, so a Pre-GA feature exercised in an isolated non-production project on synthetic data processes nothing those terms leave unprotected. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  GA status is determined only from Google's release notes and product documentation with the Pre-GA Offerings Terms disclaimer removed; console availability, marketing pages, partner training material, and press announcements are not authoritative. Where sources disagree, the more restrictive stage governs, and a product-level GA announcement does not make GA the individual features it lists. The suite spans four documentation trees, three with independent release notes (CX Agent Studio, CX Insights, and Agent Assist), so confirm GA status per component and per feature rather than for the suite. Evaluation of a Pre-GA feature is permitted only in a non-production project that holds synthetic or otherwise non-personal data, shares no tool target, data store, service agent grant, or network path with production, and on which no production process depends. Keep a register of Pre-GA features under evaluation and of those blocked, and re-verify both at every release-note review cycle. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/resources/release-notes; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/release-notes; https://cloud.google.com/terms/service-terms; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/agent-assist/release-notes; https://cloud.google.com/security/compliance/hipaa |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: SA-8 (Security and Privacy Engineering Principles), CM-3 (Configuration Change Control), CM-7 (Least Functionality), SA-4 (Acquisition Process) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000020.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | Commerce agents **MUST NOT** be deployed. |
| Rationale | Google's own documentation states that Commerce agents are not yet released: the product page ends "Commerce agents coming soon." An unreleased product has no published security documentation, no encryption or residency configuration surface, and no IAM model to assess. If it is first released as a preview, it will fall under the Pre-GA Offerings Terms and under Google's HIPAA guidance: "Do not use Pre-GA offerings ... in connection with PHI". The umbrella covered-products entry for Gemini Enterprise for Customer Experience does not by itself establish that a component with no GA release can be used with PHI. The intended capability set compounds this: Google describes Commerce agents as executing consented actions on a customer's behalf, which is a write-capable autonomous surface reaching commerce and order systems. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  This prohibition is a specific application of this guide's baseline prohibition on Non-GA and Pre-GA features, and stands until Commerce agents reach GA, are confirmed by Google as within the BAA-covered scope of Gemini Enterprise for CX, and are assessed under a fresh capability certification. Track the product page and the CX Agent Studio release notes for the transition. Nothing in this guide anticipates the control surface Commerce agents will present. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/commerce-agents; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx; https://cloud.google.com/terms/service-terms; https://cloud.google.com/security/compliance/hipaa |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: CM-7 (Least Functionality), SA-4 (Acquisition Process), SA-8 (Security and Privacy Engineering Principles), RA-3 (Risk Assessment) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000030.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | The `v1beta` CX Agent Studio API and the `v1alpha1` CX Insights API **MUST NOT** be called from production workloads. |
| Rationale | Both product APIs publish a pre-release version alongside the stable one: the CX Agent Studio REST overview lists v1 and v1beta, and the CX Insights REST reference lists v1alpha1 resources. Google does not state the launch stage of these versions on the reference pages. A beta or alpha version is conventionally a pre-release surface, so this guide treats both as Pre-GA as a matter of organizational policy until Google states their launch stage in writing; the policy does not assert that either version is outside the BAA, it removes production dependence on an unconfirmed answer. A client library or integration pinned to v1beta or v1alpha1 would otherwise place production conversation traffic on a surface whose support and compliance coverage are unconfirmed, and the version is a substring of an endpoint path that no console screen displays. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  Audit deployed client configuration, not console settings: the version appears in the endpoint path and in generated client stubs. CX Agent Studio audit log entries record the API version in the method name, for example google.cloud.ces.v1beta.EvaluationService.RunEvaluation, so a Cloud Logging query for method names containing v1beta or v1alpha1 is the detective control. Scope that query to runtime and integration principals, because some administrative methods, such as UpdateSecuritySettings, are recorded under v1beta method names and are not production workloads. Some evaluation operations are only offered on v1beta - run those in a non-production project that holds synthetic data and shares nothing with production, rather than promoting the version. The Pre-GA treatment of v1beta and v1alpha1 is a policy position drawn from the version naming rather than from a Google statement; ask Google for the launch stage and record the answer in the certification evidence. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/audit-logging; https://cloud.google.com/terms/service-terms; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/reference/rest; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/reference/rest |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: CM-7 (Least Functionality), CM-6 (Configuration Settings), SA-4 (Acquisition Process), SA-8 (Security and Privacy Engineering Principles) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000040.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | The Google Maps tool in CX Agent Studio **MUST NOT** be enabled on any agent application. |
| Rationale | The tools overview flags the Google Maps tool as Preview while presenting it in the same tool picker as generally available tool types. A builder selecting it from that list receives no signal that the resulting agent has left the supported configuration, and the tool sends conversation-derived location context to a mapping surface outside the guide's assessed boundary. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  This is a specific application of this guide's baseline prohibition on Non-GA and Pre-GA features, restated because the console does not segregate Preview tools from GA tools in the picker. Enforce by tool-type review during agent approval rather than by expecting builders to notice the marker. At the 2026-09-24 review the only other tool type marked Preview on the overview page was the A2A Protocol tool; re-check the page at each review because the picker composition changes without a release note. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/tool; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/resources/release-notes; https://cloud.google.com/terms/service-terms |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: CM-7 (Least Functionality), CM-3 (Configuration Change Control), SA-4 (Acquisition Process) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000050.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | CX Insights capabilities documented as Preview, including fine-grained access control, conversation datasets, and multiple scorecards, **MUST NOT** be used as the control of record or as a production dependency. |
| Rationale | The CX Insights release notes place several capabilities in Preview, and one of them - fine-grained access control - is the only mechanism the product offers for scoping an analyst to a subset of conversations. Building an access model on a Pre-GA feature means the boundary protecting member conversation content is one that Google may change or withdraw without notice and does not warrant under the compliance terms. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  The practical consequence is carried by the CX Insights access requirement in this guide: because fine-grained access control cannot be the control of record, project-level CX Insights access must be compensated by a deliberately small approved group plus Data Access audit logging. Do not read this requirement as prohibiting evaluation of authorized views in a non-production project - it prohibits depending on them for a production access decision. The Preview designation of conversation datasets (release note of 2025-08-15) and multiple scorecards (release note of 2025-07-18) is established only by the CX Insights release notes; their feature pages carry no Preview banner, and no GA announcement has followed. Do not read the absence of a banner on a feature page as a change of launch stage. The appearance of authorized view set and dataset resources in the v1 REST reference likewise does not make them GA. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/release-notes; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/best-practices-for-fine-grained-access-control; https://cloud.google.com/terms/service-terms; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/datasets; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/qai-basics |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(4) (Information Access Management), 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: AC-3 (Access Enforcement), CM-7 (Least Functionality), SA-4 (Acquisition Process), RA-3 (Risk Assessment); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.v (Information Access Restriction) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000060.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | A capability certification review **MUST** be completed and approved before any Gemini Enterprise for CX component processes production member conversation data. |
| Rationale | Every component of this suite reads or writes unstructured member conversation, which is among the densest concentrations of protected health information the organization holds, and several of its configuration decisions cannot be reversed after first use. Certification is the gate that forces the irreversible decisions - encryption specification, residency region, project boundary, perimeter membership, retention - to be made deliberately rather than discovered after data has landed. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Med |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  Sequence the certification before the first agent application, the first CX Insights location initialization, and the first conversation ingestion. Google states that encryption key settings for a CX Insights location cannot be changed once CMEK is enabled and cannot be configured if CX Insights data already exists, and that existing CX Agent Studio resources cannot be CMEK integrated retroactively, with export and restore into a new project as the remedy. The residency region is fixed by the location specified when resources are created. Conversation TTL can be changed later and conversations can be deleted, but conversations ingested before a TTL is set remain until deleted. A certification that runs after a pilot has ingested real conversations therefore cannot remediate the encryption decisions without rebuilding the resources. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx; https://cloud.google.com/security/compliance/hipaa; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/cmek; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/cmek; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/reference/region; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/ttl |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(1)(ii)(A) (Risk Analysis), 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: CA-2 (Control Assessments), RA-3 (Risk Assessment), SA-8 (Security and Privacy Engineering Principles), PL-8 (Security and Privacy Architectures); NIST AI RMF (AI 100-1): GOVERN, MAP, MEASURE, MANAGE |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000070.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | Newly released Gemini Enterprise for CX features **MUST** be reviewed and explicitly approved before enablement, on a review cadence no longer than monthly. |
| Rationale | The suite ships features continuously across three independent release-note streams, and the CX Agent Studio release notes alone record twelve feature entries between general availability on February 4, 2026 and July 1, 2026. New features become available as they ship rather than on an organization-controlled schedule, so an unreviewed release can become a production configuration change that no one authorized. Agents as tools and traffic splitting are both examples of additions that materially changed the authorization and rollout surface. |
| Estimated Security Value | Med |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Med |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  Assign a named owner for each of the three release-note streams - CX Agent Studio, CX Insights, and Agent Assist - and for the suite overview page, which has no release notes of its own but is where a new component such as Commerce agents first appears. Tool types can also appear in the CX Agent Studio tools overview without a release note (File search and Widget tools were found this way on 2026-09-23), so reconcile the tools overview at the same review. The review asks three questions per entry - does it change what data leaves the boundary, does it change what identity an action runs as, and does it change a setting this guide pins. Record the review outcome so the certification evidence stays current between annual reassessments. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/resources/release-notes; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/release-notes; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/agent-assist/release-notes |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(8) (Evaluation); NIST 800-53: CM-3 (Configuration Change Control), CM-4 (Impact Analyses), CA-7 (Continuous Monitoring) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000080.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| :--------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requirement                  | The configuration baseline for every Gemini Enterprise for CX project  **MUST** be documented and drift-checked at least quarterly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Rationale                    | GECX scope includes guardrails, tools, deployment channels, encryption, residency, retention, and audit settings. Almost every control in this guide is a project or agent setting that a holder of the relevant editor role can change without a deployment, a code review, or a change ticket. The ten-role IAM family means several different people can each alter one facet of the security posture independently, so the aggregate configuration is nobody's job to know unless a baseline records what it is supposed to be. |
| Estimated Security Value     | Med                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Med                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Notes                        | Capture the baseline as configuration exported through the API rather than as console screenshots, so the comparison can be automated. The high-value drift signals are guardrail level changes, new tool registrations, a deployment channel gaining public access, and a change to security settings - all four appear in Admin Activity or Data Access audit logs and can be alerted on directly.                                                                                                                                |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/audit-logging                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Mappings                     | NIST 800-53: CM-2 (Baseline Configuration), CM-3 (Configuration Change Control), CM-6 (Configuration Settings), CA-7 (Continuous Monitoring)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000090.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| :--------------------------- | :-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requirement                  | Personnel who build, deploy, or administer Gemini Enterprise for CX agent applications **SHOULD** complete role-specific training covering the confused-deputy tool execution model and the irreversible configuration decisions documented in this guide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Rationale                    | The two failure modes most likely to produce a material incident in this product are both invisible in the builder's workflow: a tool executes with the service agent's permissions, or with an identity configured on the tool, rather than the caller's, and several encryption and residency settings cannot be changed after first use. Neither is surfaced by the console at the moment the decision is made, so a builder who has not been told will not infer them from the interface.                                                                                                 |
| Estimated Security Value     | Med                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Notes                        | The training does not need to be lengthy. It needs to convey four things: tools commonly run as the CX Agent Studio service agent or another identity configured on the tool rather than as the end user, data store retrieval has no documented mechanism for applying the end user's permissions, the project boundary is the real security boundary, and CMEK and residency close at first use. The source material is the tool execution model, the data store retrieval model, the CX Insights encryption specification, the CX Agent Studio CMEK integration, and the residency region. |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/tool/open-api; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/cmek; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/insights/cmek                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Mappings                     | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(5) (Security Awareness and Training); NIST 800-53: AT-2 (Literacy Training and Awareness), AT-3 (Role-based Training), SA-8 (Security and Privacy Engineering Principles)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| CSP Level Controls           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

## Identity & Access Management

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000100.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| :--------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requirement                  | Administrative and builder access to CX Agent Studio, CX Insights, and Agent Assist **MUST** be federated through Highmark's enterprise identity provider (Entra ID).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Rationale                    | Federation is the trust anchor that extends existing conditional access policy, multi-factor enforcement, joiner-mover-leaver lifecycle, and session control to this suite. Without it, access to a surface holding member conversation content depends on a separate identity store whose deprovisioning is not tied to the HR record, leaving orphaned accounts with standing access to conversation transcripts after a person leaves.                  |
| Estimated Security Value     | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Med                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Notes                        | Federate the Google Cloud identity used to reach the console and the APIs, not just the console, either through Cloud Identity or Google Workspace single sign-on with the enterprise IdP or through Workforce Identity Federation. Confirm that the API path is covered as well, because a service account key or a personal gcloud credential bypasses federation entirely, which is why service account keys are separately prohibited for these roles. |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx; https://cloud.google.com/iam/docs/workforce-identity-federation; https://cloud.google.com/architecture/identity/single-sign-on                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Mappings                     | HIPAA: 164.312(d) (Person or Entity Authentication), 164.308(a)(3) (Workforce Security); NIST 800-53: IA-2 (Identification and Authentication (Organizational Users)), IA-5 (Authenticator Management), AC-17 (Remote Access); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.q (User Identification and Authentication), 01.j (User Authentication for External Connections)                                                                                                         |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| CSP Level Controls           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Monitoring Controls          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000110.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | Multi-factor authentication **MUST** be enforced for every human user account holding any CX Agent Studio (`roles/ces.*`) or CX Insights (`roles/contactcenterinsights.*`) role. |
| Rationale | A single compromised builder credential is sufficient to register a tool that reaches a backend system, to change a guardrail level, or to read conversation transcripts in bulk, because these roles operate on a surface that has no second approval step. Credential theft through phishing or reuse is the dominant initial access vector, and MFA is the control that breaks it. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  Enforce at the identity provider so the requirement covers console, gcloud, and API access uniformly. Google notes that super admins can bypass single sign-on, so enable Google two-step verification for super-admin accounts as well. Phishing-resistant factors are preferred for `roles/ces.admin`, `roles/ces.appEditor`, `roles/ces.securitySettingsEditor`, `roles/ces.guardrailsEditor`, `roles/ces.toolsEditor`, and `roles/ces.deploymentEditor`, which are the roles that can change the security posture or push to production. Service accounts and service agents that hold these roles cannot complete an interactive second factor and are outside this requirement; govern them through least-privilege grants, a prohibition on user-managed keys, and workload identity. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://cloud.google.com/architecture/identity/single-sign-on; https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/best-practices-service-accounts |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.312(d) (Person or Entity Authentication); NIST 800-53: IA-2(1) (Multi-factor Authentication to Privileged Accounts), IA-2 (Identification and Authentication (Organizational Users)), IA-5 (Authenticator Management); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.q (User Identification and Authentication) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID | SG-GCP-GECX-000120.0 |
| :--- | :--- |
| Requirement | The `roles/ces.admin` role **MUST** be granted only to named individual administrators. |
| Rationale | The admin role provides full access to CX Agent Studio resources, which means it collapses the ten-role separation the product otherwise offers into a single grant covering apps, agents, tools, guardrails, evaluations, security settings, and deployments. A group grant makes the effective administrator population a function of group membership managed elsewhere, so the number of people who can silently alter the security posture is unknown to the people accountable for it. |
| Estimated Security Value | High |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low |
| Audit Procedures |  |
| Notes |  Google publishes ten CX Agent Studio roles precisely so that admin does not have to be the default. Grant appEditor, agentEditor, toolsEditor, guardrailsEditor, evalsEditor, securitySettingsEditor, or deploymentEditor to match the actual job. Reserve admin for a small named set and include it in the quarterly entitlement recertification. Group grants do not satisfy this requirement. The admin role also carries every CX Insights permission - Google's product access-control page lists the CX Insights Admin role (`roles/contactcenterinsights.admin`) as its base role - so it grants full access to CX Insights conversations as well as CX Agent Studio resources. |
| References | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/ces |
| Mappings | HIPAA: 164.312(a)(1) (Access Control), 164.308(a)(4) (Information Access Management); NIST 800-53: AC-6 (Least Privilege), AC-6(1) (Authorize Access to Security Functions), AC-2 (Account Management), AC-3 (Access Enforcement); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.c (Privilege Management) |
| Detective Policies |  |
| Sentinel Modules |  |
| CSP Level Controls |   |
| Monitoring Controls |  |

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000130.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requirement                  | Basic IAM roles - the legacy Owner, Editor, and Viewer roles and the Admin, Writer, and Reader basic roles - **MUST NOT** be granted on any project hosting a Gemini Enterprise for CX component.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| Rationale                    | Basic roles predate the product's own role family and, in Google's words, "include thousands of permissions across all Google Cloud services", including `ces.googleapis.com` and `contactcenterinsights.googleapis.com`. A project Viewer therefore reads conversation content, and a project Editor registers tools and changes guardrails, without either grant appearing in any CX-specific access review. Google's own guidance is: "In production environments, do not grant basic roles unless there is no alternative."                                          |
| Estimated Security Value     | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Notes                        | Audit existing bindings before enabling the APIs, because a pre-existing project Editor binding becomes a CX Agent Studio editor binding the moment the API is turned on. This is one of the reasons this guide requires a dedicated project per trust domain rather than reuse of an existing one. Google documents that only owners of the associated project can create a WhatsApp or Instagram deployment; that is not an exception to this requirement, and the channel stays unavailable until Google documents a creation path that does not need the Owner role. |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-overview; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/deploy/whatsapp                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Mappings                     | HIPAA: 164.312(a)(1) (Access Control); NIST 800-53: AC-6 (Least Privilege), AC-3 (Access Enforcement), AC-2 (Account Management); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.c (Privilege Management)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| CSP Level Controls           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Monitoring Controls          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000140.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |     |
| :--------------------------- | :----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --- |
| Requirement                  | The `roles/ces.securitySettingsEditor` role **MUST** be held by a different set of individuals than those holding `roles/ces.agentEditor` or `roles/ces.toolsEditor`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |     |
| Rationale                    | The project Security Settings resource holds a single control, the endpoint control policy: the allowed origins list and its enforcement scope, which together decide which HTTP(S) endpoints tools and callbacks may call. Tool editors choose where a tool sends conversation-derived parameters, and agent editors do the same for callbacks. Where one person holds both sides, the same identity can register a tool or callback pointing at a new host and add that host to the allowed origins, opening an egress path for member data with no second party in the path. The product's role split makes this separation available at no operational cost.                     |     |
| Estimated Security Value     | Med                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |     |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |     |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |     |
| Notes                        | This is a separation-of-duties control, not a least-privilege control - it constrains combinations rather than individual grants. Implement it as a check in the access-request workflow and as a periodic query over IAM policy bindings, because IAM itself will not prevent the combination. The Security Settings resource carries no logging, redaction, or export settings; those are agent application settings changed with the `ces.apps.update` permission, which `roles/ces.appEditor` and `roles/ces.admin` hold, so this separation does not protect them. `roles/ces.admin` holds every permission on both sides and is governed by the admin role's own restrictions. |     |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/reference/rest/v1beta/SecuritySettings; https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/ces                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |     |
| Mappings                     | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(3) (Workforce Security), 164.312(a)(1) (Access Control); NIST 800-53: AC-5 (Separation of Duties), AC-6 (Least Privilege), AC-6(1) (Authorize Access to Security Functions); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.c (Privilege Management)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |     |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |     |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |     |
| CSP Level Controls           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |     |
| Monitoring Controls          |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |     |

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000150.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| :--------------------------- | :--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requirement                  | The `roles/ces.deploymentEditor` role **MUST** be restricted to personnel explicitly authorized to promote changes to production. Preferably a DevSecOps assignment.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Rationale                    | Deployment is the only step between an agent configuration and live member traffic. Because the product supports traffic splitting, which divides user conversations across different agent application versions within a single deployment channel, a deployment editor can also direct a share of production conversations to a different agent version without changing the agent itself.                                                                     |
| Estimated Security Value     | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Notes                        | Traffic splitting must never direct production traffic to an unreviewed agent version. Deployment create, update, and delete are DATA_WRITE methods, so they appear in Data Access audit logs rather than Admin Activity logs; enable Data Access audit logging for `ces.googleapis.com` and alert on those methods. Agent builders do not receive this role by default; where a builder also promotes, the grant is a named exception with a recorded approver. |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/deploy/traffic-split; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/audit-logging; https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/ces                                                                                                                                       |
| Mappings                     | HIPAA: 164.308(a)(3) (Workforce Security); NIST 800-53: AC-5 (Separation of Duties), AC-6 (Least Privilege), CM-3 (Configuration Change Control), CM-5 (Access Restrictions for Change); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.c (Privilege Management)                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| CSP Level Controls           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| ID                           | SG-GCP-GECX-000160.0                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| :--------------------------- | :---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Requirement                  | Direct bindings of the `roles/ces.client` role **MUST** be limited to the service identities used by approved deployment channels and to the documented end-user principal set. The principal set is tied to an approved OAuth2 deployment channel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Rationale                    | The client role provides query access to agents - its permissions are `ces.sessions.runSession`, `ces.sessions.bidiRunSession`, and `ces.tools.execute` - which is the runtime conversation path rather than an administrative one. It is the role a self-hosted token broker's service account holds, so every direct grant of it is effectively a new channel through which conversations can be initiated and tools executed. Granting it to a broad group makes the set of parties who can converse with a production agent, and therefore reach whatever its tools reach, unbounded.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Estimated Security Value     | High                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Estimated Operational Impact | Low                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| Audit Procedures             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Notes                        | Inventory direct client role bindings alongside the approved deployment channel inventory - every direct binding should map to an approved channel, and one that does not is either an undocumented integration or a leftover from testing. Google includes the client role as a base role of admin, agentEditor, toolsEditor, guardrailsEditor, and evalsEditor, so builders hold its permissions through those roles; govern them through the builder role requirements rather than this inventory. Where a web widget uses the OAuth2 option, end users sign in and their own credentials are used to access the agent application; treat that principal set as an approved channel with a documented owner rather than as an ad hoc grant. Inbound A2A callers, where the A2A Protocol is enabled, also require this role and are channels in the same sense. |
| References                   | https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/access-control; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/deploy/web-widget; https://docs.cloud.google.com/gemini-enterprise-cx/cx-agent-studio/deploy; https://docs.cloud.google.com/iam/docs/roles-permissions/ces                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Mappings                     | HIPAA: 164.312(a)(1) (Access Control), 164.312(d) (Person or Entity Authentication); NIST 800-53: AC-3 (Access Enforcement), AC-6 (Least Privilege), IA-9 (Service Identification and Authentication), AC-2 (Account Management); HITRUST CSF v11: 01.c (Privilege Management), 01.q (User Identification and Authentication)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Detective Policies           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Sentinel Modules             |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| CSP Level Controls           |                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
